Let me try a different approach - optimizing the original design with better synthesis-friendly coding and perhaps a hybrid adder approach.


  -> create_file(filename='design_opt6.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized original design with 8-bit group carry-lookahead adder\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 8-bit group carry-lookahead adder (4 groups for 32-bit)\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   logic [ACCW/8:0] group_carry;\n   assign group_carry[0] = 1'b0;\n   \n   genvar grp;\n   generate\n      for (grp = 0; grp < ACCW/8; grp++) begin : cla_8bit_groups\n         localparam int BASE = grp * 8;\n         \n         // Generate/propagate for 8 bits\n         logic [7:0] g, p;\n         assign g = psum_north[BASE+7:BASE] & prod_sext[BASE+7:BASE];\n         assign p = psum_north[BASE+7:BASE] ^ prod_sext[BASE+7:BASE];\n         \n         // Group generate/propagate\n         logic gg, gp;\n         assign gg = g[7] | (p[7] & (g[6] | (p[6] & (g[5] | (p[5] & (g[4] | (p[4] & (g[3] | (p[3] & (g[2] | (p[2] & (g[1] | (p[1] & g[0]))))))))))));\n         assign gp = &p;\n         \n         // Carry into group\n         logic cin;\n         assign cin = group_carry[grp];\n         \n         // Carry out of group\n         assign group_carry[grp+1] = gg | (gp & cin);\n         \n         // Internal carries - computed in parallel\n         logic [7:0] c_int;\n         assign c_int[0] = cin;\n         assign c_int[1] = g[0] | (p[0] & cin);\n         assign c_int[2] = g[1] | (p[1] & c_int[1]);\n         assign c_int[3] = g[2] | (p[2] & c_int[2]);\n         assign c_int[4] = g[3] | (p[3] & c_int[3]);\n         assign c_int[5] = g[4] | (p[4] & c_int[4]);\n         assign c_int[6] = g[5] | (p[5] & c_int[5]);\n         assign c_int[7] = g[6] | (p[6] & c_int[6]);\n         \n         // Sum bits\n         assign sum[BASE+7:BASE] = p ^ c_int;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

Let me try a 16-bit group CLA for better area-delay tradeoff.


  -> create_file(filename='design_opt7.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 16-bit group carry-lookahead adder (2 groups for 32-bit)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 16-bit group carry-lookahead adder (2 groups for 32-bit)\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   logic [1:0] group_carry;\n   assign group_carry[0] = 1'b0;\n   \n   genvar grp;\n   generate\n      for (grp = 0; grp < 2; grp++) begin : cla_16bit_groups\n         localparam int BASE = grp * 16;\n         \n         // Generate/propagate for 16 bits\n         logic [15:0] g, p;\n         assign g = psum_north[BASE+15:BASE] & prod_sext[BASE+15:BASE];\n         assign p = psum_north[BASE+15:BASE] ^ prod_sext[BASE+15:BASE];\n         \n         // Group generate/propagate - computed hierarchically\n         logic [3:0] gg_4, gp_4;\n         genvar sg;\n         generate\n            for (sg = 0; sg < 4; sg++) begin : sub_groups\n               localparam int SBASE = sg * 4;\n               logic [3:0] sg_g, sg_p;\n               assign sg_g = g[SBASE+3:SBASE];\n               assign sg_p = p[SBASE+3:SBASE];\n               assign gg_4[sg] = sg_g[3] | (sg_p[3] & (sg_g[2] | (sg_p[2] & (sg_g[1] | (sg_p[1] & sg_g[0])))));\n               assign gp_4[sg] = &sg_p;\n            end\n         endgenerate\n         \n         logic gg, gp;\n         assign gg = gg_4[3] | (gp_4[3] & (gg_4[2] | (gp_4[2] & (gg_4[1] | (gp_4[1] & gg_4[0])))));\n         assign gp = &gp_4;\n         \n         // Carry into group\n         logic cin;\n         assign cin = group_carry[grp];\n         \n         // Carry out of group\n         assign group_carry[grp+1] = gg | (gp & cin);\n         \n         // Internal carries - 4-bit sub-groups\n         logic [15:0] c_int;\n         assign c_int[0] = cin;\n         \n         // First 4-bit sub-group\n         assign c_int[1] = g[0] | (p[0] & cin);\n         assign c_int[2] = g[1] | (p[1] & c_int[1]);\n         assign c_int[3] = g[2] | (p[2] & c_int[2]);\n         assign c_int[4] = g[3] | (p[3] & c_int[3]);\n         \n         // Second 4-bit sub-group\n         logic c4;\n         assign c4 = gg_4[0] | (gp_4[0] & cin);\n         assign c_int[5] = g[4] | (p[4] & c4);\n         assign c_int[6] = g[5] | (p[5] & c_int[5]);\n         assign c_int[7] = g[6] | (p[6] & c_int[6]);\n         assign c_int[8] = g[7] | (p[7] & c_int[7]);\n         \n         // Third 4-bit sub-group\n         logic c8;\n         assign c8 = gg_4[1] | (gp_4[1] & c4);\n         assign c_int[9]  = g[8]  | (p[8]  & c8);\n         assign c_int[10] = g[9]  | (p[9]  & c_int[9]);\n         assign c_int[11] = g[10] | (p[10] & c_int[10]);\n         assign c_int[12] = g[11] | (p[11] & c_int[11]);\n         \n         // Fourth 4-bit sub-group\n         logic c12;\n         assign c12 = gg_4[2] | (gp_4[2] & c8);\n         assign c_int[13] = g[12] | (p[12] & c12);\n         assign c_int[14] = g[13] | (p[13] & c_int[13]);\n         assign c_int[15] = g[14] | (p[14] & c_int[14]);\n         \n         // Sum bits\n         assign sum[BASE+15:BASE] = p ^ c_int;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt9.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with carry-select adder (two 16-bit ripple adders + mux)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // Carry-select adder: two 16-bit ripple adders (cin=0 and cin=1) + mux\n   logic [15:0] sum_lo_0, sum_lo_1;\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi_0, sum_hi_1;\n   logic        carry_lo_0, carry_lo_1;\n   \n   // Lower 16 bits with cin=0\n   assign {carry_lo_0, sum_lo_0} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0] + 1'b0;\n   // Lower 16 bits with cin=1\n   assign {carry_lo_1, sum_lo_1} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0] + 1'b1;\n   \n   // Upper 16 bits with cin=0\n   assign sum_hi_0 = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + 1'b0;\n   // Upper 16 bits with cin=1\n   assign sum_hi_1 = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + 1'b1;\n   \n   // Select upper sum based on lower carry\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi;\n   assign sum_hi = carry_lo_0 ? sum_hi_1 : sum_hi_0;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo_0};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt10.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 2-stage pipelined adder for better timing\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 2-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into two 16-bit adds with registered carry\n   logic [15:0] sum_lo;\n   logic        carry_lo;\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi;\n   \n   // Stage 1: Lower 16 bits\n   assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: Upper 16 bits with carry from lower\n   assign sum_hi = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + carry_lo;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt11.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 4-bit group carry-lookahead adder (8 groups for 32-bit)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 4-bit group carry-lookahead adder (8 groups for 32-bit)\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   logic [ACCW/4:0] group_carry;\n   assign group_carry[0] = 1'b0;\n   \n   genvar grp;\n   generate\n      for (grp = 0; grp < ACCW/4; grp++) begin : cla_4bit_groups\n         localparam int BASE = grp * 4;\n         \n         // Generate/propagate for 4 bits\n         logic [3:0] g, p;\n         assign g = psum_north[BASE+3:BASE] & prod_sext[BASE+3:BASE];\n         assign p = psum_north[BASE+3:BASE] ^ prod_sext[BASE+3:BASE];\n         \n         // Group generate/propagate\n         logic gg, gp;\n         assign gg = g[3] | (p[3] & (g[2] | (p[2] & (g[1] | (p[1] & g[0])))));\n         assign gp = &p;\n         \n         // Carry into group\n         logic cin;\n         assign cin = group_carry[grp];\n         \n         // Carry out of group\n         assign group_carry[grp+1] = gg | (gp & cin);\n         \n         // Internal carries\n         logic [3:0] c_int;\n         assign c_int[0] = cin;\n         assign c_int[1] = g[0] | (p[0] & cin);\n         assign c_int[2] = g[1] | (p[1] & c_int[1]);\n         assign c_int[3] = g[2] | (p[2] & c_int[2]);\n         \n         // Sum bits\n         assign sum[BASE+3:BASE] = p ^ c_int;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt12.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 4-stage pipelined adder (8+8+8+8 split)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 4-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into four 8-bit adds with registered carries\n   logic [7:0] sum_0, sum_1, sum_2, sum_3;\n   logic       carry_0, carry_1, carry_2;\n   \n   // Stage 1: bits 0-7\n   assign {carry_0, sum_0} = psum_north[7:0] + prod_sext[7:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: bits 8-15\n   assign {carry_1, sum_1} = psum_north[15:8] + prod_sext[15:8] + carry_0;\n   \n   // Stage 3: bits 16-23\n   assign {carry_2, sum_2} = psum_north[23:16] + prod_sext[23:16] + carry_1;\n   \n   // Stage 4: bits 24-31\n   assign sum_3 = psum_north[31:24] + prod_sext[31:24] + carry_2;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_3, sum_2, sum_1, sum_0};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

MXU)\n// Optimized with 2-stage pipelined adder + optimized skew/deskew\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 2-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into two 16-bit adds with registered carry\n   logic [15:0] sum_lo;\n   logic        carry_lo;\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi;\n   \n   // Stage 1: Lower 16 bits\n   assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: Upper 16 bits with carry from lower\n   assign sum_hi = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + carry_lo;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: optimized with shared shift registers\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   // Use a single 2D shift register array for all rows\n   logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   \n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    skew_sr[i][k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  skew_sr[i][0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    skew_sr[i][k] <= skew_sr[i][k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[i][i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: optimized with shared shift registers\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-2];\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    deskew_sr[j][k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  deskew_sr[j][0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    deskew_sr[j][k] <= deskew_sr[j][k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[j][D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 3-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into 11+11+10 for better balance\n   logic [10:0] sum_0;\n   logic        carry_0;\n   logic [10:0] sum_1;\n   logic        carry_1;\n   logic [9:0]  sum_2;\n   \n   // Stage 1: bits 0-10 (11 bits)\n   assign {carry_0, sum_0} = psum_north[10:0] + prod_sext[10:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: bits 11-21 (11 bits)\n   assign {carry_1, sum_1} = psum_north[21:11] + prod_sext[21:11] + carry_0;\n   \n   // Stage 3: bits 22-31 (10 bits)\n   assign sum_2 = psum_north[31:22] + prod_sext[31:22] + carry_1;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_2, sum_1, sum_0};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt15.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 8-bit group CLA + tree-based group carry (4 groups)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 8-bit group CLA with tree-based group carry (4 groups of 8 bits)\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   logic [3:0] gg, gp;  // group generate/propagate\n   logic [3:0] gc;      // group carries\n   logic [3:0] c_int;   // intermediate carries\n   \n   genvar grp;\n   generate\n      for (grp = 0; grp < 4; grp++) begin : cla_8bit_groups\n         localparam int BASE = grp * 8;\n         \n         // Generate/propagate for 8 bits\n         logic [7:0] g, p;\n         assign g = psum_north[BASE+7:BASE] & prod_sext[BASE+7:BASE];\n         assign p = psum_north[BASE+7:BASE] ^ prod_sext[BASE+7:BASE];\n         \n         // Group generate/propagate\n         logic [2:0] gg_2, gp_2;\n         assign gg_2[0] = g[1] | (p[1] & g[0]);\n         assign gp_2[0] = p[1] & p[0];\n         assign gg_2[1] = g[3] | (p[3] & (g[2] | (p[2] & gg_2[0])));\n         assign gp_2[1] = p[3] & p[2] & gp_2[0];\n         assign gg_2[2] = g[5] | (p[5] & (g[4] | (p[4] & gg_2[1])));\n         assign gp_2[2] = p[5] & p[4] & gp_2[1];\n         \n         assign gg[grp] = g[7] | (p[7] & (g[6] | (p[6] & gg_2[2])));\n         assign gp[grp] = p[7] & p[6] & gp_2[2];\n         \n         // Internal carries (will be computed after group carries)\n         logic [7:0] c;\n         assign c[0] = (grp == 0) ? 1'b0 : gc[grp-1];\n         assign c[1] = g[0] | (p[0] & c[0]);\n         assign c[2] = g[1] | (p[1] & c[1]);\n         assign c[3] = g[2] | (p[2] & c[2]);\n         assign c[4] = g[3] | (p[3] & c[3]);\n         assign c[5] = g[4] | (p[4] & c[4]);\n         assign c[6] = g[5] | (p[5] & c[5]);\n         assign c[7] = g[6] | (p[6] & c[6]);\n         \n         assign sum[BASE+7:BASE] = p ^ c;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   \n   // Tree-based group carry computation\n   assign gc[0] = gg[0];\n   assign gc[1] = gg[1] | (gp[1] & gc[0]);\n   assign gc[2] = gg[2] | (gp[2] & gc[1]);\n   assign gc[3] = gg[3] | (gp[3] & gc[2]);\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")
