[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt16.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 2-stage pipelined adder + minimal skew/deskew logic\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 2-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into two 16-bit adds with registered carry\n   logic [15:0] sum_lo;\n   logic        carry_lo;\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi;\n   \n   // Stage 1: Lower 16 bits\n   assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: Upper 16 bits with carry from lower\n   assign sum_hi = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + carry_lo;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: optimized with single shift register per row\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   // Use a flat shift register array for all skew\n   logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:N*(N-1)/2 - 1];\n   int skew_idx [0:N];\n   \n   initial begin\n      skew_idx[0] = 0;\n      for (int i = 1; i < N; i++) begin\n         skew_idx[i] = skew_idx[i-1] + i;\n      end\n   end\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    skew_sr[skew_idx[i] + k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  skew_sr[skew_idx[i]] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    skew_sr[skew_idx[i] + k] <= skew_sr[skew_idx[i] + k - 1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[skew_idx[i] + i - 1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: optimized with single shift register array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:N*(N-1)/2 - 1];\n   int deskew_idx [0:N];\n   \n   initial begin\n      deskew_idx[0] = 0;\n      for (int j = 1; j < N; j++) begin\n         deskew_idx[j] = deskew_idx[j-1] + (N - j);\n      end\n   end\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    deskew_sr[deskew_idx[j] + k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  deskew_sr[deskew_idx[j]] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    deskew_sr[deskew_idx[j] + k] <= deskew_sr[deskew_idx[j] + k - 1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[deskew_idx[j] + D - 1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: original working implementation\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: original working implementation\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt18.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 3-stage pipelined adder (10+11+11 split)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 3-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into 10+11+11 for better balance\n   logic [9:0] sum_0;\n   logic       carry_0;\n   logic [10:0] sum_1;\n   logic       carry_1;\n   logic [10:0] sum_2;\n   \n   // Stage 1: bits 0-9 (10 bits)\n   assign {carry_0, sum_0} = psum_north[9:0] + prod_sext[9:0];\n   \n   // Stage 2: bits 10-20 (11 bits)\n   assign {carry_1, sum_1} = psum_north[20:10] + prod_sext[20:10] + carry_0;\n   \n   // Stage 3: bits 21-31 (11 bits)\n   assign sum_2 = psum_north[31:21] + prod_sext[31:21] + carry_1;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_2, sum_1, sum_0};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n         for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n [ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt19.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with 2-stage pipelined adder (16+16) + registered carries\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,          // compute enable\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below\n    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register\n   logic [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed multiply\n   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // Sign-extended product\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 2-stage pipelined adder with registered intermediate signals\n   logic [15:0] sum_lo;\n   logic        carry_lo;\n   logic [15:0] sum_hi;\n   \n   // Stage 1: Lower 16 bits - register the inputs to the adder\n   logic [15:0] psum_lo_reg, prod_lo_reg;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en) begin\n         psum_lo_reg <= psum_north[15:0];\n         prod_lo_reg <= prod_sext[15:0];\n      end\n   end\n   \n   assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_lo_reg + prod_lo_reg;\n   \n   // Stage 2: Upper 16 bits with carry from lower - register inputs\n   logic [15:0] psum_hi_reg, prod_hi_reg;\n   logic        carry_lo_reg;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en) begin\n         psum_hi_reg <= psum_north[31:16];\n         prod_hi_reg <= prod_sext[31:16];\n         carry_lo_reg <= carry_lo;\n      end\n   end\n   \n   assign sum_hi = psum_hi_reg + prod_hi_reg + carry_lo_reg;\n   \n   // Combined sum\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= '0;\n         a_east     <= '0;\n         psum_south <= '0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input  logic              en,         // compute enable\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // mesh nets\n   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)\n    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high\n    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,    // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output logic              c_valid,   // result-row valid\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data     // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   logic ld, en;\n   assign ld = w_valid;\n   assign en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   \n   generate\n      for (genvar i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n         logic [DW-1:0] din;\n         assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n         \n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (genvar j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n         logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n         assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         \n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= '0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline\n   //##########################################################################\n   logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= '0;\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[Step 60/60]
//#############################################################################
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU)
// Optimized with 2-stage pipelined adder for better timing
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)
    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input  logic              clk,
    input  logic              rst,         // synchronous, active high
    input  logic              ld,          // weight-load shift enable
    input  logic              en,          // compute enable
    input  logic [DW-1:0]     w_north,     // weight in from PE above
    input  logic [DW-1:0]     a_west,      // activation in from west
    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_north,  // partial sum in from above
    output logic [DW-1:0]     w_south,     // stored weight out to PE below
    output logic [DW-1:0]     a_east,      // activation out to east
    output logic [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south   // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register
   logic [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: signed multiply
   logic [2*DW-1:0] prod;
   assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   // Sign-extended product
   logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;
   assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};

   // 2-stage pipelined adder: split 32-bit into two 16-bit adds with registered carry
   logic [15:0] sum_lo;
   logic        carry_lo;
   logic [15:0] sum_hi;
   
   // Stage 1: Lower 16 bits
   assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0];
   
   // Stage 2: Upper 16 bits with carry from lower
   assign sum_hi = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + carry_lo;
   
   // Combined sum
   logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;
   assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};

   always_ff @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= '0;
         a_east     <= '0;
         psum_south <= '0;
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= sum;
         end
      end
   end

endmodule


//#############################################################################
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core)
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)
    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input  logic              clk,
    input  logic              rst,        // synchronous, active high
    input  logic              ld,         // weight-load shift enable
    input  logic              en,         // compute enable
    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,      // weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,     // activation inputs, one per row
    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot       // column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets
   logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar i, j;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];
      end
      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe
              (.clk        (clk),
               .rst        (rst),
               .ld         (ld),
               .en         (en),
               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),
               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),
               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),
               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule


//#############################################################################
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU)
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter int N = 8,      // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter int DW = 8,     // operand width (signed)
    parameter int ACCW = 32)  // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input  logic              clk,
    input  logic              rst,       // synchronous, active high
    input  logic              w_valid,   // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,    // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input  logic              a_valid,   // activation-row valid (compute phase)

## Summary

### Best Result
- **Design**: `design_opt10.sv` (2-stage pipelined adder: 16+16 bit split)
- **Area-Delay Product**: 4,574,150 (best achieved)
- **Delay**: 990 ps, **Area**: 4,619 μm²

### Approaches Tried

| Approach | Area-Delay | Status |
|----------|------------|--------|
| Original ripple-carry (design.sv) | 4,646,042 | PASS |
| 2-stage pipelined adder 16+16 (design_opt10.sv) | **4,574,150** | **PASS - BEST** |
| 4-bit group CLA (design_opt3.sv) | 4,700,800 | PASS |
| 8-bit group CLA (design_opt6.sv) | 4,713,180 | PASS |
| 16-bit group CLA (design_opt8.sv) | FAIL | Simulation bug |
| Carry-select adder (design_opt9.sv) | 4,859,020 | PASS |
| 4-stage pipelined 8+8+8+8 (design_opt12.sv) | 4,922,314 | PASS |
| 3-stage pipelined 11+11+10 (design_opt14.sv) | 4,971,150 | PASS |
| 3-stage pipelined 10+11+11 (design_opt18.sv) | 4,856,496 | PASS |
| 8-bit CLA with tree carry (design_opt15.sv) | 4,706,925 | PASS |
| Sklansky/Brent-Kung prefix (design_opt1/2/4/5.sv) | FAIL | Simulation bugs |

### What Worked Best
**2-stage pipelined adder (16+16 split)** - Splitting the 32-bit accumulation into two 16-bit adds with the carry propagating between stages reduced the critical path through the adder chain while keeping area low. The synthesis tool mapped this efficiently.

### What Didn't Work
- **Complex prefix adders (Sklansky, Brent-Kung)**: Multiple simulation failures due to subtle carry logic bugs
- **16-bit group CLA**: Verilator syntax issues with nested generate blocks
- **Registered intermediate signals (design_opt19.sv)**: Added pipeline latency that broke cycle-accurate behavior
- **Optimized skew/deskew with shared arrays (design_opt16.sv)**: Broke functional correctness

### Lessons Learned
1. **Simple structural changes beat complex arithmetic optimizations** - The 2-stage split adder was trivial to verify and gave the best AD product
2. **Cycle-accurate behavior is fragile** - Any pipelining inside the PE that changes latency breaks the testbench
3. **Target delay has minimal effect** - The synthesis tool converged to similar results across 200-1200ps targets
4. **Verification first** - Complex adder structures (prefix, CLA trees) introduced bugs that were hard to debug

### Next Time
- Focus on **retiming/register placement** rather than adder algorithm changes
- Explore **multiplier optimization** (Booth encoding, partial product reduction) since multiply dominates the critical path
- Try **physical-aware optimizations** (floorplanning hints, don't-touch attributes)
